# Motion index: every action and gait style, by type and by legs

Every action (7,064 in 27 sets) and every gait style (3,383 in 18 libraries) of `system.bin`, indexed so that you can choose: **actions by TYPE** (attack, hit, die, defend, jump, idle ...) **and by LEGS** (2, 4, 6, any body); **gait styles by LEGS and TYPE** (walk, run, trot, gallop ...). Generated by `system/motion_index.js` of `happy` (not kept here); do not edit by hand.

## How to choose

1. **Count the legs of the creature** (`2` a person, a bird, a robot, a dinosaur on two legs; `4` an animal; `6` an insect). Take actions and gait styles of its legs, or of `any body` (the hand-made presets: a track of a part the body lacks is left out, so they play on anything).
2. **Find the type** in the index below and read the line of your legs: each line is `set (how many): names`. A name that ends in `↻` loops until it is stopped, one that ends in `‖` is held (a pose); the others play once.
3. **Use it by name.** An action: `api.action("<set>::<name>")` in the creature's function (`me.act(h)`), or `sp.worker_send` to a function that plays it; the set is only needed when two sets have the same name. A gait style: `legs: { gait: { from: "<name>" } }` in the creature file, `sp.set_style("<name>")` while it walks, or `api.style("<name>")` in its function. A style's speed is in **leg lengths a second**: multiply by the creature's leg length for metres a second (0.9 leg lengths is 0.8 m/s on a person's 0.86 m legs).
4. **Try it.** The creature demo and the action player of `happy` (not kept here) put any creature file on a floor and play any of these on it.

Things worth knowing:

* The sets named `learned_*` repeat an action of the set they are named after with the moments measured in it (windup, strike, step, takeoff, land, grab) and are named `<name>_ev`: choose those when a game needs the moment of a blow, the sets without `_ev` for the plain motion.
* An action is played on whatever body the creature has: the tracks of a part the body lacks (an arm of a wolf, a tail of a person) are left out, so a person's action on an animal plays with what it has and shows little. Actions of the right legs look right; the others are for trying.
* A gait style is a recording of a body with that many legs. A creature is *given* any style (the order of its steps is made again for its own legs, its legs' lengths make the numbers its own) but the stride, the foot's path and the upper body are those of the body it was recorded on: take styles with **as many legs as the creature has**, and try the others.
* The type of an action and of a style is **derived** (from the kind, the tags and the words of the name: the rules are at the end of this file), so an odd one may sit in a type you would not give it: use the search of your editor on a name.

### Gait styles by legs and type

| legs | walk | crouch | march | carry | injured | turn | jog | run | sprint | trot | pace | bound | gallop | pronk | hop | tripod | other | all |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| [2 legs](#gait-styles-2-legs) | 1,282 | 59 | 52 | 63 | 24 | 21 | 80 | 277 | 54 | · | · | · | · | · | 97 | · | · | 2,009 |
| [4 legs](#gait-styles-4-legs) | 484 | 42 | · | · | 2 | · | 2 | 46 | 10 | 183 | 24 | 355 | 85 | 80 | · | · | · | 1,313 |
| [6 legs](#gait-styles-6-legs) | · | · | · | · | · | · | · | · | · | · | · | · | · | · | · | 13 | 48 | 61 |

What a gait type is: **walk** a plain walk; **crouch** a walk crouched low, sneaking, creeping or crawling; **march** a march, a strut, a catwalk: a walk with a manner; **carry** a walk that carries or does something; **injured** a limp, a hobble, an injured or tired walk; **turn** walking round a turn; **jog** a jog; **run** a run; **sprint** a sprint: five leg lengths a second or more on two legs; **trot** the two diagonal legs step together (four legs); **pace** the two legs of a side step together (four legs); **bound** the hind legs and the front legs go as pairs, with a flight between (four legs); **gallop** the four legs strike one after the other (four legs); **pronk** all four legs push off together; **hop** both legs push off together, a hop; **tripod** an insect's walk: three legs at a time; **other** a pattern that is none of the others.

## Actions

Each line: **legs** · `set` (how many): names, in the order of the alphabet. `↻` loops, `‖` is held.

### Actions: attack

1,462 actions: a blow, a shot or a spell that is meant to hurt: the action has a moment where it lands (events `hit`, `swing`, `shoot` ...).

## How the types are made

**An action's legs:** the tag `N legs` of its description (the animal corpus: 2, 4, 6), else `person` (a two legged body), else, for a hand-made preset, `any body`, else the legs its tracks move (a front leg: 4; only the hind legs: 2; none: any body).

**An action's type:** `die` if its words say death, dying or killed; for an **attack** the kind says it and the words say which (fists: punch, jab, hook, uppercut, cross, boxing; kicks and knees; blades: swords, daggers, fencing, slash, stab; guns: rifle, pistol, shotgun, minigun, shoot; magic; creature: bite, claw, headbutt, tail, pounce ...); of a **reaction**: `defend` (block, guard, dodge, shield), else `hit` (hit, pain, damage, hurt, stun, flinch), else `fear` (fear, hide, panic), else `react`; a **movement** is `jump` if it jumps or leaps and `move` otherwise; a **pose** is `rest` (rest, sleep, lie), `sit`, `crouch` (crouch, kneel) or `pose`; idle, gesture, social, dance and locomotion are the kinds of those names; an **activity** is eating and drinking, throwing, work or play.

**A gait style's legs** are the limbs its `phase` names. **Its type** is its pattern when it has one that says (trot, pace, bound, gallop, pronk, hop, tripod), else, for an alternating walk, its tags and name: `sprint` (a run of five leg lengths a second or more), `run`, `jog`, `crouch` (crouched, creeping, sneak, crawl), `injured` (limp, hobble, tired, hurt, drunk), `march` (march, strut, catwalk), `carry` (carry, pick, hold, phone, coffee), `turn`, else `walk`.

The counts: 7,064 actions, of which 1,792 loop and 289 are held; 3,383 gait styles.
